*Nima Eshraghi,Lovedeep Gondara,Yuqing Huang,Sagarika Suresh,Leizer Teran,Jithin Pradeep,Xiaotong Xu,Fanny Chevalier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出随机令牌引导（STS）和随机块引导（SBS），通过稀疏随机干预替代密集引导，在保持流畅性的同时恢复大部分引导效果，发现SAE介导的控制受速率限制，行为结果取决于序列上的累积信号剂量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准激活引导方法在每个生成令牌上都施加引导信号，导致恒定的逐令牌扰动，可能损害生成流畅性。作者质疑这种密集干预是否必要，旨在通过随机稀疏干预降低计算开销和流畅性损失。

Method: 提出两种方法：STS（每个令牌独立以概率p门控）和SBS（每个序列在起始窗口引导一次），无需奖励模型或学习门控策略。通过改变干预比例和引导幅度，研究SAE介导控制的行为输出与累积信号剂量的关系。

Result: 在两种模型家族和两个行为任务上，仅引导50%的令牌即可恢复大部分密集引导效果并保持流畅性，引导30%的令牌即可超越基于提示的控制。最优引导幅度与干预比例呈反比。

Conclusion: SAE介导的控制是速率受限的：行为结果取决于序列上的累积信号剂量，而非逐令牌的密集干预。随机稀疏引导是一种高效且有效的替代方案。

Abstract: Activation steering via sparse autoencoders (SAEs) enables behavioral control of large language models without task-specific fine-tuning, but standard methods apply the steering signal at every generated token, incurring constant per-token perturbation that risks degrading fluency. We ask: is dense intervention necessary? We introduce Stochastic Token Steering (STS), which gates each token independently with probability $p$, and Stochastic Block Steering (SBS), which gates a leading window once per sequence; neither requires a reward model or learned gating policy. Across two model families and two behavioral tasks, steering only 50% of the tokens recovers most of the dense-steering effect while preserving fluency, and steering as few as 30% surpasses prompt-based control. The optimal steering magnitude scales inversely with the intervention ratio, revealing that SAE-mediated control is rate-limited: the behavioral outcome depends on cumulative signal dosage across a sequence.

</details>


### [53] [Safe Bayesian Optimization with Counterfactual Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05620)
*Katherine Avery,Bruno Castro da Silva,David Jensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合保形预测和安全贝叶斯优化的方法，用于在反事实基线未知的情况下，确保新干预措施不降低既定阈值下的结果，并控制约束违例率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多决策场景（如临床医学）中，新干预措施只有在不降低既定标准下的结果时才可接受。然而，基线策略的结果是反事实的、不可观测的，因此需要估计这些反事实结果并利用其不确定性来安全地优化目标。

Method: 使用保形预测构建反事实基线结果的有效不确定性区间，并将这些区间整合到安全贝叶斯优化中，以确保障碍违例率低于用户指定的阈值。同时，还适配了不同种类的协变量偏移。

Result: 提供了安全性证明、实验证据和敏感性分析。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效利用反事实估计的不确定性，在控制违例率的前提下安全地进行贝叶斯优化。

Abstract: In many decision-making settings, new interventions are acceptable only if they do not reduce outcomes below some established threshold. For example, in clinical medicine, new treatments are often acceptable only if they do not worsen outcomes relative to an established standard of care. Safe Bayesian optimization maximizes an objective subject to safety constraints. In the setting that we consider here, safety is defined relative to a known baseline policy whose outcomes are counterfactual and therefore unobserved. Thus, the counterfactual outcomes of the baseline policy must be estimated and those (uncertain) estimates must be used to safely optimize the objective. We address this estimation problem by using conformal prediction to construct valid uncertainty intervals for counterfactual baseline outcomes, and we show how these intervals can be integrated into safe Bayesian optimization to ensure that constraint violations occur at or below a user-specified rate. We also show how to adapt these conformal estimates to different kinds of covariate shift. We provide a safety proof, experimental evidence, and a sensitivity analysis.

</details>


### [54] [Intuitionistic Fuzzy Graph Embedded Random Vector Functional Link with Multiview Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05635)
*Vrushank Ahire,Yogesh Kumar,M. A. Ganaie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种直觉模糊图嵌入随机向量函数链接多视图学习模型（IFGRVFL-MV），通过结合直觉模糊集、图嵌入和多视图学习，解决了RVFL网络在保持几何关系和利用多特征视图方面的局限性，在UCI和KEEL基准数据集上分类精度优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RVFL网络虽然训练快速且具有通用逼近能力，但在保持几何关系（图结构）和有效利用多个特征视图方面存在挑战。

Method: 提出IFGRVFL-MV模型，包含三个关键组件：(1)直觉模糊集，为数据点分配隶属度和非隶属度值以处理不确定性并对抗异常值；(2)图嵌入框架，保留数据的拓扑结构以提升泛化性能；(3)多视图学习，利用多个特征空间的互补信息。

Result: 在UCI和KEEL基准数据集上的实验表明，IFGRVFL-MV在分类精度上优于现有模型。

Conclusion: IFGRVFL-MV在不确定性和多视图环境下是一个有前景的进展。

Abstract: Random Vector Functional Link (RVFL) networks are popular due to their fast training and universal approximation capabilities. However, RVFL models face challenges in preserving geometric relationships and utilizing multiple feature views effectively. To address these limitations we propose the Intuitionistic Fuzzy Graph Embedded Random Vector Functional Link with Multiview Learning (IFGRVFL-MV) model. The proposed approach comprises three key components: intuitionistic fuzzy sets for uncertainty handling, graph embedding to capture intrinsic geometric structures, and multiview learning to use complementary information from multiple feature spaces. The model assigns intuitionistic fuzzy membership and non-membership values to data points making it robust to outliers. Also, the graph embedding framework preserves topological structures, increasing the generalization performance. We performed experiments on benchmark datasets from UCI and KEEL repositories which concludes that IFGRVFL-MV outperforms existing models in classification accuracy. Our results establish that IFGRVFL-MV is a promising advancement in the domain of uncertainty and multiview environments.

</details>


### [55] [Domain-Adaptive Climate Downscaling Under Temporal Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05645)
*Shuochen Wang,Nishant Yadav,Auroop R. Ganguly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对深度学习降尺度模型在历史与未来气候分布存在时间分布偏移的问题，提出时间域自适应降尺度框架，通过域对齐提升未来高分辨率气候投影的鲁棒性，实验表明该方法在分布偏移最强时表现最优，尤其在高海拔和地形复杂区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习降尺度模型通常基于历史数据训练，但应用于未来气候投影时面临时间上的分布外（OOD）挑战——未来气候分布可能与训练期存在显著差异，导致模型性能下降。

Method: 提出时间域自适应降尺度框架，包含两部分：1）基于历史数据的监督高分辨率重建；2）历史与未来气候分布之间的域对齐（domain alignment）。

Result: 该域自适应模型在所有未来验证期一致优于统计和深度学习的偏差校正方法；分布偏移最强时性能增益最大；在高海拔和地形复杂区域改善更显著；域自适应还降低了上尾温度偏差。

Conclusion: 时间域自适应能够提升非平稳气候条件下高分辨率气候投影的鲁棒性，有效应对时间分布偏移带来的OOD挑战。

Abstract: Deep-learning-based climate downscaling aims to learn relationships from historical low-resolution (LR) and high-resolution (HR) climate data to generate HR climate projections. However, this setting faces a temporal out-of-distribution (OOD) challenge: models trained on historical data are commonly applied to future projections whose distributions may differ substantially from the training period. This study investigates temporal OOD shift for daily temperature downscaling over the Continental United States using paired LR-HR model simulations. We propose a temporal domain-adaptive downscaling framework that combines supervised HR reconstruction on historical data with domain alignment between historical and future climate distributions. Experiments across future validation periods show that the proposed domain-adaptive model consistently outperforms statistical and deep-learning-based bias-correction methods, with the largest gains occurring when the temporal distribution shift is strongest. Spatial analyses indicate stronger improvements over high-elevation and topographically complex regions, along with higher spatiotemporal correlation with the HR target. The extreme analysis shows that domain adaptation also reduces upper-tail temperature bias relative to the non-adaptive model. These results demonstrate that temporal domain adaptation can improve the robustness of HR climate projections under non-stationary climate conditions.

</details>


### [56] [LLM-Driven Neural Network Generation with Same-Family Architecture Guidance: Disentangling Transfer and Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05704)
*Kabir Dev Paul Baghel,Radu Timofte,Dmitry Ignatov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种源引导的候选生成协议，利用更强同族源模型改进弱目标模型。该协议区分有效性与准确性，仅在提升目标时选择最佳有效候选。实验表明，该方法在CIFAR-10和SVHN上显著优于非源候选和直接复制源配方，AlexNet和alt_nn1族系中表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型可生成神经网络修改，但无约束生成常导致无效或有害结果。本文旨在限制该设置，利用神经网络数据库中更强的同族源模型来改进弱目标模型。

Method: 提出源引导的候选生成协议：包含非源控制、源条件候选和无LLM的hp_copy消融，在相等评估预算下分别报告有效性与准确性，仅当有效候选提升目标时才选择最佳结果。

Result: CIFAR-10上源引导候选准确率0.5049 vs 非源候选0.2398（+0.2651），弱目标原始0.1254；五轮检查保持增益0.7686 vs 0.4839。SVHN AlexNet上源引导0.7880 vs 0.2254（+0.5626）；重复实验0.8069 vs 0.2509（+0.5560）。直接复制源配方仅0.1959，而hp_transfer达0.7880，说明LLM是适应而非复制。族系分析中AlexNet 6/8胜，alt_nn1 8/10胜。

Conclusion: 源引导的协议能有效利用更强同族模型改进弱目标，LLM并非简单复制源配方而是进行适应调整，该方法在特定族系中表现尤为突出。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate neural-network modifications, but unrestricted generation is often invalid or harmful. This paper studies a narrower setting: improving a weak target model using a stronger same-family source model from a neural-network database. We propose a source-guided candidate-generation protocol with non-source controls, source-conditioned candidates, and a no-LLM hp_copy ablation under equal evaluation budgets. The protocol reports validity separately from accuracy and selects the best valid candidate only when it improves the target. On CIFAR-10, the strongest source-guided candidate reaches 0.5049 accuracy versus 0.2398 for the best non-source candidate, a +0.2651 advantage, while improving a weak target originally at 0.1254; a five-epoch check preserves the gain at 0.7686 versus 0.4839. On SVHN AlexNet with DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B, source-guided transfer reaches 0.7880 versus 0.2254, a +0.5626 advantage; a fresh repeat reaches 0.8069 versus 0.2509, a +0.5560 advantage. Direct source-recipe copy produces 0.1959 on SVHN AlexNet, matching the original target, while hp_transfer reaches 0.7880, showing that the LLM adapts rather than copies the source recipe. Family-level analysis shows the clearest positive signals for AlexNet, with 6/8 wins across SVHN, Imagenette, and CelebA-Gender, and alt_nn1, with 8/10 wins on CIFAR-10.

</details>


### [57] [FourTune: Towards Fully 4-Bit Efficient Post-Training for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05711)
*Bowen Xue,Zihan Min,Xingyang Li,Zhekai Zhang,Haocheng Xi,Lvmin Zhang,Maneesh Agrawala,Jun-Yan Zhu,Song Han,Yujun Lin,Muyang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FourTune是一个面向扩散模型的高效后训练框架，采用W4A4G4量化范式，通过三支混合流水线（含冻结数值稳定器）和硬件高效块级量化及定制融合内核，在FLUX.1-dev（12B）上相比BF16 LoRA减少2.25倍内存开销并提升2.27倍训练吞吐量，同时保持全精度微调的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型后训练面临显存占用高、训练速度慢的挑战，已有的参数高效微调方法仅能部分解决这些问题，亟需更高效的方案来适配多样化下游应用。

Method: 提出FourTune框架，采用端到端W4A4G4量化范式；设计三支混合流水线，在标准LoRA架构上增加冻结数值稳定器以隔离量化敏感异常值；采用硬件高效块级量化和定制融合内核，支持高效量化反向传播并降低内存带宽开销。

Result: 在定制化、强化学习和蒸馏任务上，FourTune匹配全精度微调的质量；在FLUX.1-dev（12B）上，相比BF16 LoRA，内存开销降低2.25倍，端到端训练吞吐量提升2.27倍。

Conclusion: FourTune通过量化感知的混合训练流水线和硬件优化内核，有效解决了扩散模型后训练的效率瓶颈，在保持生成质量的同时实现了显著的显存节约和加速。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become a dominant paradigm for high-quality generative modeling, while post-training is essential for adapting them to diverse downstream applications. However, post-training of large diffusion models is still challenging due to the prohibitive memory footprints and slow training speed, which existing parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods only partially address. To overcome these limitations, we propose FourTune, an efficient post-training framework for diffusion models based on an end-to-end W4A4G4 paradigm. FourTune introduces a triple-branch hybrid pipeline that augments the standard LoRA architecture with a frozen numerical stabilizer to isolate quantization-sensitive outliers, enabling stable training under native 4-bit computation. In addition, FourTune employs hardware-efficient block-wise quantization and customized fused kernels to support efficient quantized backpropagation and reduce memory bandwidth overhead. Across customization, reinforcement learning, and distillation tasks, FourTune matches the quality of full-precision fine-tuning. On FLUX.1-dev (12B), FourTune reduces memory overhead by 2.25$\times$ and increases end-to-end training throughput by 2.27$\times$ compared to BF16 LoRA.

</details>


### [58] [Low-Overhead Error-Corrected QCNNs Using Bivariate Bicycle Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05724)
*Alejandro Rosales,Animesh Yadav*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于距离-4双变量循环（BB）码的量子纠错技术，用于保护量子卷积神经网络（QCNN）免受噪声影响。实验表明，未受保护的4量子比特QCNN在真实硬件噪声下无法收敛，而所提的BB码纠错方案以较低开销实现了有效保护，推动了QCNN的实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子设备噪声水平过高，导致QCNN无法实际执行；同时，虽然表面码等纠错方案有效，但其量子比特开销过大。因此，需要一种低开销且有效的量子纠错方案来保护QCNN。

Method: 采用双变量循环（bivariate bicycle, BB）码作为量子纠错方案，利用其高错误阈值、恒定编码率和线性码距离等特性，为QCNN设计距离-4的量子纠错技术。通过模拟真实硬件噪声源，对比未受保护与受保护QCNN的性能。

Result: 未受保护的4量子比特QCNN在真实硬件噪声下无法收敛，且学习率低于数值模拟理想情况；所提出的BB码纠错方案有效提升了QCNN在噪声环境下的训练性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的低开销BB码量子纠错技术是使QCNN走向实际应用的重要一步。

Abstract: Quantum convolutional neural networks (QCNNs) combine the power of quantum computing and classical CNN for computational speedup in classification tasks. However, noise levels on state-of-the-art quantum devices remain too high for practical QCNN execution. In addition, despite the reliable surface code providing a method for error rates below a threshold value, they have a prohibitively large qubit cost. Recently introduced bivariate bicycle (BB) codes are of particular interest for their high error threshold, constant encoding rate, and linear code distance. Through simulation with realistic hardware noise sources, we demonstrate that a 4-qubit unprotected QCNN fails to converge and exhibits a worse learning rate compared to numerical simulations. Addressing both limitations, we propose a distance-4 BB quantum error-correction (QEC) technique for QCNNs. In doing so, we validate that our low-overhead QEC technique for QCNNS represents a step toward practical QCNNs.

</details>


### [59] [Multimodal Molecular Representation Learning with Graph Neural Networks, Deep & Cross Networks, and SMILES Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05736)
*Qiwei Han,Chi Zhou,Ruobing Wang,Zheng Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种三分支模块融合神经网络（Tri-Branch Modular Fusion Neural Network），通过融合3D空间几何（SchNet）、离散拓扑语法（SMILES via ChemBERTa）和显式宏观物理化学描述符（Deep & Cross Network）三种正交模态，在QM9数据集上以不到百万参数实现了0.0207 eV的验证MAE，较几何基线降低了20.6%的误差，证明了多模态融合作为O(1)物理捷径的效力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分子性质预测依赖单一数据模态，连续3D图神经网络难以高效捕获长程拓扑依赖和精确的宏观启发式信息，存在算术与过平滑限制。

Method: 提出三分支模块融合神经网络：SchNet处理3D空间几何、ChemBERTa处理SMILES离散拓扑语法、Deep & Cross Network处理显式宏观物理化学描述符，采用共享后期融合架构构建多模态潜在空间。

Result: 在QM9数据集上针对$U_0^{\mathrm{atom}}$性质，验证MAE为0.0207 eV，参数量少于百万，较几何基线降低20.6%误差，超越次化学精度阈值。

Conclusion: 整合正交宏观与拓扑数据流提供了$\mathcal{O}(1)$物理捷径，为高通量虚拟筛选（HTVS）管道提供了高效的替代方案，无需暴力参数缩放。

Abstract: Molecular property prediction often relies on isolated data modalities, where continuous 3D graph neural networks (GNNs) struggle to efficiently capture long-range topological dependencies and exact macroscopic heuristics. In this work, we introduce a parameter-efficient Tri-Branch Modular Fusion Neural Network that synthesizes three orthogonal modalities: 3D spatial geometry (SchNet), discrete topological grammar (SMILES via ChemBERTa), and explicit macroscopic physicochemical descriptors (Deep & Cross Network). By bypassing standard scalar readouts and employing a shared late-fusion architecture, the framework establishes a mathematically rigorous multimodal latent space that effectively resolves the arithmetic and oversmoothing limitations of local message passing. We evaluate the proposed architecture on the QM9 benchmark, targeting the extensive thermodynamic property of atomization energy at 0 K ($U_0^{\mathrm{atom}}$). Through systematic combinatorial ablation and latent bottleneck optimization ($d_e=64$), the tri-modal framework achieves a validation Mean Absolute Error (MAE) of 0.0207 eV. Operating with fewer than one million parameters, this architecture decisively surpasses the sub-chemical accuracy threshold and yields a substantial 20.6% error reduction over a strictly controlled geometric baseline. Ultimately, our findings demonstrate that integrating orthogonal macroscopic and topological data streams provides a synergistic, $\mathcal{O}(1)$ physical shortcut. This multimodal alignment offers a highly efficient alternative to brute-force parameter scaling, establishing a robust surrogate model for high-throughput virtual screening (HTVS) pipelines.

</details>


### [60] [Two Sides of the Same Coin: Learning the Backdoor to Remove the Backdoor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05748)
*Qi Zhao,Christian Wressnegger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HARVEY通过学习针对有毒样本的参考模型（而非良性样本），实现了近乎完美的后门移除，在保持自然精度的同时将攻击成功率降至最低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法通过学习良性样本的参考模型来识别后门，但学习良性参考模型困难。本文发现学习有毒参考模型更容易，从而更准确地识别有毒样本。

Method: HARVEY学习一个针对有毒（后门）样本的参考模型（oracle），而非学习良性样本的参考模型。由于学习后门参考模型比学习良性参考模型更容易，因此能更精确地识别有毒样本。

Result: HARVEY在多种攻击类型、数据集和架构上显著优于现有方法，将攻击成功率降至最低，且自然精度损失可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 通过学习有毒样本参考模型而非良性样本参考模型这一关键差异，HARVEY实现了近乎完美的后门移除，大幅超越现有反后门防御方法。

Abstract: The community has recently developed various training-time defenses to counter neural backdoors introduced through data poisoning. In light of the observation that a model learns poisonous samples responsible for the backdoor easier than benign samples, these approaches either use a fixed threshold of the training loss for splitting or iteratively learn a reference model as an oracle for identifying benign samples. In particular, the latter has proven effective for anti-backdoor learning.
  Our method, HARVEY, leverages a similar yet crucially different technique: learning an oracle for poisonous rather than benign samples. Learning a backdoored reference model is significantly easier than learning a reference model on benign data. Consequently, we can identify poisonous samples much more accurately than related work identifies benign samples. This crucial difference enables near-perfect backdoor removal as we demonstrate in our evaluation. HARVEY substantially outperforms related approaches across attack types, datasets, and architectures, lowering the attack success rate to the very minimum at a negligible loss in natural accuracy. The figure below shows an overview of our methods working principle.

</details>


### [61] [Heckman-Corrected Epistemic Uncertainty: Selection on Unobservables Defeats Importance Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05806)
*Gunner Levi Howe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将计量经济学中Heckman选择模型（1979年）引入深度学习不确定性量化，解决因不可观测变量导致的选择偏差问题。通过联合建模Probit选择方程与深度神经网络结果方程，利用逆米尔斯比率校正不可观测选择偏差。实验表明，在不可观测相关性ρ=0.9时，标准方法（深度集成、MC Dropout、GP）的90%置信区间覆盖率降至64.4%，重要性加权仅43.1%，而Heckman校正恢复至88.9%（有工具变量时）。代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准机器学习方法假设选择偏差可被观测变量解释（可忽略性假设），但在实际场景中（如贷款审批、医疗检测）选择过程常依赖不可观测因素，导致条件偏差E[y|x,selected]≠E[y|x]。现有方法（重要性加权、协变量偏移校正、MAR插补）无法解决不可观测选择偏差问题，需要引入结构化选择性偏差校正机制。

Method: 构建一个两方程联合模型：(1) 深度神经网络作为结果方程，建模目标变量y；(2) 线性Probit选择方程，建模样本被观测到的概率。两个方程通过双变量正态似然联合估计，共享相关误差项。采用深度集成（Deep Ensemble）获取预测方差。当存在工具变量（影响选择但不影响结果）时，模型可识别。实现上与经典Stata的Heckman估计量精度达到七位数一致。

Result: 在可控生成器实验中，不可观测相关性ρ=0.9时：深度集成覆盖率64.4%，MC Dropout和GP类似；重要性加权（使用真实倾向分数）仅43.1%；Heckman校正（有工具变量）恢复至88.9%；无工具变量时降至40.3%。在真实表格数据（诱导MNAR选择）中，Heckman校正区间在校准指标（region-ECE）上最优。

Conclusion: Heckman选择模型可有效弥补深度学习在不可观测选择偏差下的过度自信问题。重要性加权校正的是协变量分布而非条件偏差，无法解决E[y|x,selected]≠E[y|x]问题。工具变量的存在是关键识别条件，论文明确给出了不同识别机制下的实践指南。

Abstract: Training data for machine learning is routinely collected by a selection process the model never sees: loans are observed only when granted, outcomes only when a test was ordered. The standard fixes -- importance weighting, covariate-shift correction, MAR imputation -- assume selection is ignorable given observables. Econometrics solved the harder case in 1979: Heckman's two-equation model jointly fits a probit selection equation and an outcome equation linked through correlated errors, and the inverse-Mills-ratio term corrects for selection on unobservables, where importance weighting is structurally helpless. We instantiate this for deep epistemic uncertainty: a deep outcome network, a linear selection head, and a joint bivariate-normal likelihood over all units, ensembled for predictive variance. In a controlled generator where sampling probability depends on an unobservable correlated (rho up to 0.9) with the outcome noise, deep ensembles, MC dropout, and GP baselines are overconfident exactly where data was avoided: coverage of nominal-90% intervals falls to 64.4% at rho=0.9, and importance weighting with oracle propensities does not fix it (43.1%) -- reweighting corrects the covariate distribution, not the conditional bias E[y|x,selected] != E[y|x]. The Heckman correction restores coverage (88.9%) when the selection equation has an instrument -- a variable affecting selection but not the outcome -- and degrades measurably without one (40.3%); we chart this honesty curve rather than hide it. On real tabular data with induced MNAR selection, the corrected intervals are the best-calibrated (lowest region-ECE) non-oracle method in selected-against regions; baselines matching its raw coverage do so only by over-widening everywhere. Our estimators reproduce classic Stata output to seven digits. We state which identification regime a practitioner is in, and release the code.

</details>


### [62] [Level-Crossing Density as a Mesh-Free High-Frequency Auxiliary Loss for Implicit Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05815)
*Gunner Levi Howe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于自动微分的平滑蒙特卡洛估计器，用于计算连续神经场的闵可夫斯基泛函（面积、边界测度、欧拉示性数），在2D和3D中达到1-3%精度，比基于网格的持续同调方法快250倍。但发现了一个普遍失败模式：梯度下降会将拓扑噪声隐藏在采样密度以下，使得基于样本的拓扑目标失效，而基于网格的PH基线虽慢但无此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经场的拓扑优化依赖于持续同调（PH），计算成本高（网格PH约650-1000ms/迭代），且缺乏可微的、高效的拓扑损失函数。需要一种廉价且可微的拓扑形态描述符来替代昂贵的PH。

Method: 利用co-area公式和Gauss-Bonnet定理，从连续神经场的散点采样出发，通过自动微分推导出闵可夫斯基泛函（面积、周长、欧拉示性数）的平滑蒙特卡洛估计器。提出了四条设计规则：密集水平阶梯、C²骨干网络、完整闵可夫斯基向量、采样尺度覆盖。

Result: 2D/3D中拓扑精度达1-3%，速度约3ms/迭代（PH约650-1000ms），250倍加速。在2D拓扑修复任务中，向量化闵可夫斯基损失是唯一既能修复拓扑（3/3种子）又能保持保真度的方法。但在3D神经SDF拟合中发现了失败模式：梯度下降将噪声隐藏在采样密度以下，需要立方级增加采样点才能覆盖，成本优势消失。

Conclusion: 基于采样的拓扑估计器在速度上优势显著，但存在致命盲区——对低于采样密度的拓扑噪声不敏感。网格PH虽慢250倍，但无此空域问题。当前阶段，持续同调的昂贵成本是避免拓扑优化失效的代价。

Abstract: The Minkowski functionals of a field's excursion sets -- area, boundary measure, and Euler characteristic -- describe its level-set morphology; the Euler characteristic is the cheapest handle on topology. We derive smooth Monte-Carlo estimators for all three of a continuous neural field, evaluated at scattered points via the co-area formula and Gauss-Bonnet, using only autodiff: no grid, no complex, no persistence. The estimator is accurate to 1-3% against exact topology in 2D and 3D, and costs about 3 ms per iteration where a persistent-homology (PH) loss on a cubical grid costs 650-1000 ms -- a 250x gap. We establish four design rules without which these losses silently fail: a dense level ladder (invariants are flat in the parameters away from transitions), a $C^2$ backbone (ReLU nets hide curvature in kinks), the full Minkowski vector (Euler characteristic alone is an alternating sum, gamed by debris-hole cancellation; pricing perimeter closes the channel), and sampling-scale coverage. In 2D the vector-valued cap is the only method in a controlled comparison that both repairs topology (3/3 seeds) and preserves fidelity -- uniform smoothing repairs at 11-17x the fidelity cost, and the Euler term alone repairs nothing. In 3D neural-SDF fitting, however, a failure mode we believe general to any sampled soft topology objective appears: gradient descent adversarially hides topological noise below the sampling density, where the estimator is blind -- spurious-feature counts are invariant to 4x more samples, and closing the window needs cubically many points, erasing the cost advantage. A grid-based PH baseline, whose complex is the evaluation resolution, solves the same benchmark ($4/9$ exact; median $b_1$ error 1 vs. ours above $10^4$). The 250x cost of persistence is, at present, the price of having no null space. We release estimators, receipts, and benchmarks.

</details>


### [63] [Decision-Focused Scenario Generation and Selection for Efficient and Robust Grid Dispatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05830)
*Yangze Zhou,Yihong Zhou,Thomas Morstyn,Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向决策的生成框架，用于基于分布鲁棒优化（DRO）的电力系统调度中的相关场景生成。该框架直接根据下游运行成本优化生成场景，而非仅拟合历史分布，并适配VAE、GAN和扩散模型等主流生成模型，同时开发了可微场景选择器以提高计算效率。案例表明相比传统精度导向方法可降低0.80%-2.02%的运行成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的场景生成方法以精度为导向训练生成模型，忽视了不确定性之间的空间相关性，导致为DRO构建的模糊集虽然在统计上合理，但在下游调度运行中效果欠佳。

Method: 提出决策导向生成框架：(1) 将生成模型的训练目标从拟合历史分布改为最小化下游DRO调度运行成本；(2) 适配VAE、GAN、扩散模型三种主流生成模型，捕捉跨母线的联合不确定性分布；(3) 设计可微场景选择器，从生成池中选取与决策相关的场景，并嵌入同一决策导向训练流水线中。

Result: 在不同生成模型上，所提框架相比精度导向方法可降低0.80%-2.02%的运行成本，验证了决策导向训练的有效性。

Conclusion: 将场景生成与下游运行决策目标对齐，能够显著提升DRO调度在实际运行中的经济性，为不确定性感知的电力系统调度提供了新范式。

Abstract: The increasing uncertainty from flexible demand and renewable generation has made distributionally robust optimization (DRO) an important tool for robust power system dispatch. DRO relies on forecast scenarios to construct ambiguity sets, but conventional scenario generation pipelines are often trained in an accuracy-oriented manner and may neglect spatial correlations among uncertainties. This mismatch can produce ambiguity sets that are statistically plausible but suboptimal for downstream operation. This work proposes a decision-focused generative framework for correlated scenario generation in DRO-based dispatch. Instead of training generative models solely to fit the historical uncertainty distribution, the proposed framework optimizes generated scenarios according to their induced downstream operational cost. The proposed framework is tailored to mainstream generative models, including variational autoencoders, generative adversarial networks, and diffusion models, while capturing the joint distribution of uncertainties across buses. To improve computational tractability, we further develop a differentiable scenario selector that selects decision-relevant scenarios from a generated pool and can be trained within the same decision-focused pipeline. Case studies demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively reduces 0.80%-2.02% operational cost across different generative models compared to accuracy-oriented methods.

</details>


### [64] [AbICL: In-Context Learning for Antigen-Specific Antibody Affinity Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05846)
*Zhiyuan Chen,Jing Hu,Junzhe Wang,Yueyang Huang,Xinyi Yang,Zhaoyang Wang,Feng Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AbICL，一个基于上下文学习（ICL）的框架，用于抗原特异性抗体亲和力排序。该方法结合预训练结构编码器和上下文排序头，通过 episodic meta-training 策略利用少量标注比较样本进行测试时自适应，无需梯度更新。在AbRank基准上，AbICL一致优于现有排序方法，尤其在分布偏移和细粒度亲和力区分场景下优势明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抗体亲和力排序方法将各比较独立处理，忽略了已有标注比较中蕴含的上下文信息，难以捕获抗原特异性的结合景观。当只有少量实验表征的亲和力比较可用时，如何利用这些已有比较来推断抗原特异性排序模式，成为一个关键问题。

Method: 提出AbICL框架：1）结合预训练结构编码器提取抗体-抗原结构特征；2）设计上下文排序头（context ranking head）利用支持集样本进行推理；3）采用episodic meta-training策略训练，使模型能在无需梯度更新的情况下利用支持样本进行测试时自适应。

Result: 在AbRank基准上，AbICL在几乎所有数据划分和评估基准上一致优于现有排序基线。上下文演示的价值取决于其与目标推理任务的匹配程度，在分布偏移和细粒度亲和力区分场景下效果更为显著。

Conclusion: ICL是抗体亲和力排序的有效范式，尤其适用于单个全局排序函数不足以解决问题的挑战性场景，展示了利用少量标注样本进行抗原特异性排序的潜力。

Abstract: Accurate ranking of antibody candidates according to their binding affinity is essential for therapeutic antibody discovery. However, existing methods treat affinity comparisons independently and ignore the contextual information encoded in other labeled comparisons, limiting their ability to capture antigen-specific binding landscapes. For many target antigens, a small number of experimentally characterized affinity comparisons are often available. An important question is whether the model can exploit these existing comparisons to infer antigen-specific ranking patterns that facilitate subsequent affinity ranking. This form of learning from labeled demonstrations closely resembles the paradigm of In-Context Learning, motivating us to revisit antibody affinity ranking from an ICL perspective. To this end, we propose AbICL, an ICL framework for antigen-specific antibody affinity ranking. AbICL combines a pretrained structural encoder with a context ranking head and is trained with an episodic meta-training strategy that enables the model to leverage support demonstrations for test-time adaptation without gradient updates. Experiments on the AbRank benchmark demonstrate that AbICL consistently outperforms existing ranking baselines across almost all data splits and evaluation benchmarks. Further analysis shows that the value of contextual demonstrations depends on how well they match the target inference task, and becomes increasingly pronounced under distribution shift and fine-grained affinity discrimination. These findings highlight the potential of ICL as an effective paradigm for antigen-specific antibody affinity ranking, particularly in challenging settings where a single global ranking function is insufficient.

</details>


### [65] [Unsupervised Anomaly Detection of Information Operations Users via Behavioral and Language Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05855)
*Sishun Liu,Sajal Halder,Ke Deng,Yan Wang,Xiuzhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为TENSOR的无监督信息操作用户检测方法，通过融合时间行为（时序点过程）和语言信号（大语言模型证据函数）进行异常检测，在五个真实数据集上优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监督式IO检测方法无法捕捉IO用户行为的动态演化特性，而无监督方法依赖于IO用户间存在协调行为的过度简化假设，但实际中这种协调行为可能并不存在。

Method: 将IO用户检测问题形式化为异常检测问题，提出TENSOR方法：1) 使用时序点过程（TPP）捕捉IO用户异常的时间行为模式（如协调性发帖活动）；2) 设计新型证据函数，将基于用户帖子时间线的LLM响应转化为量化分数，用于调整TPP输出以提升检测效果。

Result: 在五个真实世界IO数据集上的实验结果表明，TENSOR方法优于所有基线方法。

Conclusion: TENSOR通过多模态数据（时间行为+文本内容）的无监督异常检测框架，有效克服了现有方法的局限性，为信息操作用户检测提供了更实用且准确的解决方案。

Abstract: Information Operations on social media networks have been identified as a significant threat to democracy and modern society, but they are challenging and expensive to detect by humans. Existing supervised IO detection methods fail to capture the dynamic nature of evolving IO user behavior, while existing unsupervised approaches rely on oversimplified assumptions of coordination among IO users that may not exist in practice. To overcome the limitations of existing methods, we formulate IO user detection as an anomaly detection problem and propose a novel unsupervised IO user detection approach called Temporal-bEhavior-laNguage Signals for information Operation Recognition (TENSOR), which leverages multimodal data, including temporal online user behavior, such as message posting activities, and the textual content of the messages. The motivation is that IO users are typically a very small fraction of all online users and have unique temporal behavioral and language patterns. Specifically, we train a Temporal Point Process (TPP) to capture abnormal temporal behavioral patterns of IO users because they are known to behave in a coordinated manner for IO campaigns. We further introduce a novel evidence function that converts LLM responses, which are generated from user post timelines, into quantitative scores to adjust the TPP outputs for better IO user detection. Experimental results show that TENSOR outperforms the baselines on five real-world IO datasets. Code is available at https://github.com/xiuzhenzhang/TENSOR.

</details>


### [66] [Strategic Bargaining in Multi-Buyer Markets: Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards for LLM Negotiations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05863)
*Shuze Daniel Liu,Claire Chen,Jiabao Sean Xiao,Xin Chen,David Simchi-Levi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）训练大型语言模型，使其在并发多买家谈判场景中学会平衡市场探索与盈余提取，从而超越标准LLM的表现，提取更高盈余并泛化至未见场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LLM在经济学决策中表现不佳，在并发多买家谈判中缺乏市场探索策略，倾向于固守当前最高出价而非战略性调查市场以发现潜在高估值买家。

Method: 提出基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）训练框架，将奖励函数锚定于客观经济结果（如最终盈余），使战略平衡（市场探索与盈余提取）通过学习过程自然涌现。

Result: 训练后的卖家经历多阶段战略演化，学会利用价格锚定和战略探查识别更有利可图的交易对手；相比前沿模型提取显著更高的盈余；策略能稳健泛化至未见买家谈判风格和预算分布。

Conclusion: RLVR训练框架能有效赋予LLM经济决策能力，使其在复杂谈判场景中同时提升说服性议价技能和与高价值买家达成交易的能力。

Abstract: Negotiation is a fundamental strategic interaction in management science, characterized by agents attempting to reach agreements while protecting private information, such as reservation costs and hidden valuations. A prevalent yet complex scenario involves a single seller negotiating concurrently with multiple buyers, each possessing heterogeneous, private budgets. In such settings, constrained by a limited number of communication turns, the seller must balance exploring the broader market to discover the highest valuation with concentrating sufficient turns on a single target buyer to secure the best possible outcome. Our analysis reveals a significant gap in standard Large Language Models (LLMs): while these models are linguistically proficient, they fail to act as effective economic decision-makers. Specifically, they exhibit a failure to explore the buyer pool, often fixating on the current highest bid rather than strategically investigating the market to discover latent high valuations.
  In this paper, we propose a specialized training recipe using Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR). By anchoring the reward function to objective economic outcomes, the strategic balance between market discovery and surplus extraction emerges natively through the learning process. Our results demonstrate that the trained seller undergoes a multi-stage strategic evolution, learning to leverage price anchoring and strategic probing to identify more profitable counterparties. The agent extracts a substantially higher surplus than frontier models by both improving its persuasive bargaining skills and consistently closing deals with high-value buyers. Finally, we show that our seller strategies generalize robustly to unseen buyer negotiation styles and budget distributions.

</details>


### [67] [Differentially Private Natural Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05866)
*Pan Li,Kai Chen,Shuai Chang,Shengzhi Zhang,Peizhuo Lv,Jinwen He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DP-NGD，通过将自然梯度下降与差分隐私结合，在不增加隐私预算消耗的情况下利用损失曲率信息，实现了比一阶DP优化器（如DP-SGD）更优的模型效用和高达10倍的收敛加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准一阶DP优化器（如DP-SGD）仅依赖局部梯度，忽略损失曲率信息，导致在病态条件下严重锯齿振荡，浪费隐私预算。现有方法陷入两难：要么提前停止训练，要么注入大量每步噪声，两者都严重损害最终模型效用。

Method: DP-NGD框架通过三项关键技术解决NGD与DP集成的挑战：（1）将曲率估计与私有数据解耦以避免隐私预算消耗；（2）通过白化空间机制调和各向同性DP约束与各向异性二阶优化；（3）动态钳制曲率以稳定训练。

Result: 在标准基准测试上，DP-NGD实现了最先进的精度，突破了基于一阶基线的效用上限，在相同隐私预算下实现了高达10倍的收敛加速。

Conclusion: DP-NGD通过将曲率信息引入DP训练，提供了打破隐私-效用瓶颈的原则性路径，证明了二阶优化在隐私保护机器学习中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Under a fixed privacy budget, the utility of differentially private (DP) training is ultimately determined by its optimization efficiency. Standard first-order DP optimizers such as DP-SGD rely solely on local gradients and ignore the underlying loss curvature. This geometric blindness causes severe zigzagging in ill-conditioned landscapes, squandering precious privacy budgets on inefficient iterations. Practitioners are thus trapped in a bind: either stop training prematurely or inject massive per-step noise, both of which critically compromise final model utility. Natural Gradient Descent (NGD) resolves this by preconditioning gradients with curvature, aligning updates with the loss geometry and extracting more efficient signal from every noisy step, offering a principled pathway to break the privacy-utility bottleneck.
  Despite its theoretical appeal, directly integrating NGD with DP introduces fundamental challenges: curvature estimation itself consumes prohibitive privacy budgets, isotropic DP operations conflict with the anisotropic scaling of NGD, and the inverse curvature catastrophically amplify parameter updates in flat directions, causing training instability. We propose DP-NGD, a practical framework that systematically addresses these obstacles by decoupling curvature estimation from private data, reconciling isotropic DP constraints with anisotropic second-order optimization via a whitened-space mechanism, and dynamically clamping the curvature to stabilize training. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that DP-NGD achieves state-of-the-art accuracy, breaking through the utility ceilings of first-order baselines while delivering up to a $10\times$ convergence speedup under the same privacy budget.

</details>


### [68] [Auditing of Unlearning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05898)
*Sahasrajit Sarmasarkar,Anastasia Koloskova,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于成员推理攻击的审计方法，通过计算数据相关的ε下限来检验机器学习模型是否真正实现了数据遗忘（unlearning）。实验表明，具有严格理论保证的算法（如模型裁剪、rewind-to-delete）的ε值很小，而经验性方法（如Hessian-based、交错上升-下降等）的ε值很大，表明遗忘效果不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遗忘算法的有效性验证缺乏统一标准，如何判定一个模型是否真正从训练数据中"遗忘"了特定样本是一个未解决的开放问题。

Method: 利用成员推理攻击，计算数据依赖的遗忘参数ε下限；基于假设检验框架，提供实证反驳遗忘声明的审计工具。

Result: 严格保证的算法（模型裁剪、rewind-to-delete）ε上界极小，不能推翻其遗忘保证；经验方法（Hessian-based、交错上升-下降、遗忘集上升、保留集微调）ε上界大，表明遗忘效果差。

Conclusion: 提出的审计方法可作为一种实用工具，用于实证检验和反驳模型遗忘声明。

Abstract: Evaluating whether unlearning algorithms truly remove training data influence remains an open challenge. We propose a practical auditor that computes data-dependent lower bounds on the unlearning parameter $\varepsilon$ using membership inference attacks. Evaluating multiple unlearning algorithms, we find a sharp separation: algorithms with rigorous guarantees, such as model clipping and rewind-to-delete, achieve very small $\varepsilon$ bounds that do not falsify their unlearning guarantees, whereas empirical methods such as Hessian-based unlearning, interleaved ascent-descent, ascent on the forget set, and fine-tuning on the retain set exhibit large bounds, indicating poor unlearning. Our auditor provides a practical tool for empirically falsifying unlearning claims through a hypothesis-testing framework, and we validate it on CIFAR-100 and Shakespeare text.

</details>


### [69] [K-ABENA: K-Adaptive Backpropagation with Error-based N-exclusion Algorithm : (Compensated Loss-Based Sample Exclusion with Unbiased Gradient Estimation)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05903)
*Jean-Francois Bonbhel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: K-ABENA是一种选择性梯度计算框架，通过在后向传播中排除部分低损失样本，结合Horvitz-Thompson逆概率重加权实现无偏梯度估计，在保证收敛性的同时节省28-54%的计算量，且分类性能与全批量SGD统计上无显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低深度学习每轮训练的计算成本，通过选择性反向传播排除低损失（"次要"）样本，同时克服早期变体（如OHEM、SBP）因选择偏差导致的性能崩溃问题。

Method: 提出K-ABENA框架，其规范形式（v3）对次要样本采用防御性混合采样设计与Horvitz-Thompson逆概率重加权，得到设计无偏的梯度估计器；自归一化变体偏差为O(1/m)量级；提供有偏"正则化模式"（半域变体）作为可选方案。

Result: 在0.17%类别不平衡下，未补偿变体测试AUC仅0.53-0.62，补偿估计器达0.9991（全批量SGD为0.9998），节省28.4%计算量；在真实数据集上节省28-54%计算，统计上与全批量SGD无差异；有偏模式在40%标签噪声下准确率跌至0.386（基线0.832）。

Conclusion: K-ABENA的补偿估计器能在显著节省计算的同时维持与全批量训练几乎一致的性能，未补偿的损失选择性方法存在根本性缺陷；所有结论均经过理论证明或实验验证。

Abstract: We present K-ABENA (K-Adaptive Backpropagation with Error-based N-exclusion Algorithm), a selective gradient computation framework that reduces per-iteration training cost by excluding a fraction of low-loss ("minor") observations from the backward pass. Its canonical form (v3) combines a defensive-mixture sampling design over the minor set with Horvitz-Thompson inverse-probability reweighting, yielding a design-unbiased Horvitz-Thompson gradient estimator (Lemma 2) and whose self-normalized practical variant carries a bias of order O(1/m) with an explicit constant (Lemma 3). We prove an O(1/sqrt(T)) non-convex convergence guarantee for SGD under the estimator, with an additive term that quantifies the residual bias (Theorem 1). We further prove that uncompensated loss-based selection - a family that includes OHEM, SBP, and the two earlier K-ABENA variants - admits no stationary point at any minimizer where its selection bias is bounded away from zero (Proposition 2), and we quantify this failure empirically: at 0.17% class imbalance, uncompensated variants reach test AUC 0.53-0.62 versus 0.9998 for full-batch SGD, while the compensated estimator attains 0.9991 at identical 28.4% compute savings. On real datasets (Breast Cancer, Digits, Wine, Diabetes) the compensated estimator is statistically indistinguishable from full-batch SGD (paired permutation tests, p >= 0.5; Section 7) while saving 28-54% of per-epoch gradient computation. A biased "regularized mode" (the earlier half-domain variant) is retained as an option with a proven exact bias decomposition (Lemma 5) and quantified contraindications: it collapses to 0.386 accuracy under 40% label noise (baseline: 0.832) and to 0.53 AUC under extreme imbalance. Every advantage and every limitation reported in this paper is either proved or measured; all experiments are CPU-scale (NumPy/scikit-learn) and their scope is stated explicitly.

</details>


### [70] [More Convincing, Not More Correct: Self-Play Reward Hacking of Reference-Free LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05904)
*Chenyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现基于自奖励/自博弈的语言模型训练存在结构性缺陷：模型作为评判者时评估的是回答的可信度（plausibility）而非正确性（correctness），导致策略可利用假阳性漏洞（false-positive basins）进行奖励黑客攻击。通过隐藏锚点审计（hidden-anchor audit）在GSM8K上验证：自博弈将评判通过率从0.72提升至0.94，但真实准确率仅0.20。解决方案是让评判者先给出自身答案再评估候选答案（committing first），可将假阳性率从0.719降至0.012，并作为训练奖励保持零假阳性。该现象在best-of-N选择和不同模型上均可复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示自奖励/自博弈/LLM-as-a-judge训练范式中的结构性缺陷——模型作为评判者时无法区分可信度与正确性，导致奖励黑客攻击，并探索有效的防御机制。

Method: 提出隐藏锚点审计（hidden-anchor audit）方法，通过跨来源精确匹配（cross-source exact-match）检测评判漏洞；比较"先承诺再评估"（committing first）与直接评估候选答案两种范式；在GSK8K、代码和竞赛数学任务上进行实验验证。

Result: 自博弈驱动评判通过率从0.72升至0.94，但真实准确率仅0.20；错误跨评判模型族（Qwen, Llama, Gemma）迁移；三元组评判集成仍接受55%错误；"先承诺再评估"将假阳性率从0.719降至0.012，盲解判别力提升至0.96，作为训练奖励保持零假阳性。

Conclusion: 自奖励训练范式存在结构性假阳性漏洞，根源在于评判者评估可信度而非正确性；"先承诺再评估"是有效的预防性措施而非仅检测手段；该漏洞可通过一个可证伪的界限（gap ≤ 1 - accuracy）预测暴露条件。

Abstract: Training a language model against its own reference-free judgments (the premise of self-rewarding, self-play, and LLM-as-a-judge pipelines) assumes a model's verdict on a shown answer tracks correctness. We show it fails structurally: conditioned on a candidate, a judge scores plausibility, not correctness, leaving false-positive basins a policy learns to exploit. We measure this with a hidden-anchor audit: a held-out, cross-source exact-match check the judge never sees. On GSM8K with Qwen3 policies, self-play drives the judge's pass rate from 0.72 to 0.94 while true accuracy stays at 0.20 (three seeds). This reward hacking is not white-box gaming: the errors transfer across judge families (Qwen, Llama, Gemma) and scales, a strict three-judge ensemble still accepts 55% of them, and no plausibility-scoring defense closes the basin. The decisive variable is whether the judge commits an answer of its own before using the candidate: committing first drops the false-positive rate from 0.719 to 0.012, blind solving lifts discrimination to 0.96, and used as the training reward the de-anchored channel keeps false positives at zero, preventing the basin rather than only detecting it. A falsifiable bound (the gap is at most 1 - accuracy) predicts which regimes are exposed. The full arc replicates without training under best-of-N selection in code and competition math, and with a Gemma policy.

</details>


### [71] [Drift Happens: An Empirical Study of Neural Architecture Robustness to Temporal Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05908)
*Robin Holzinger,Riccardo Colletti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了不同架构（MLP、CNN、RNN、预训练Transformer）在时间分布漂移下的鲁棒性，发现利用局部高判别性特征的模型（如CNN）在分布内准确率高但随时间退化最快，而预训练编码器依赖更粗粒度、更稳定的表征，退化更缓慢。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界数据分布随时间演化，导致时间分布漂移会严重降低已部署机器学习系统的可靠性，但架构选择及其归纳偏置对时间鲁棒性的影响尚未得到充分理解。

Method: 在图像分类、多标签文本分类和文本回归三个异构时间索引领域进行系统实证比较，使用基于时间漂移矩阵的统一评估框架，在累积历史数据上训练模型并评估其在早期和晚期时间段的性能，跨模型家族（MLP、CNN、RNN、预训练Transformer）进行量化。

Result: 架构归纳偏置系统地塑造了时间鲁棒性：利用局部高判别性特征的模型分布内准确率最高，但这些特征随时间变化最快，因此模型退化最快；预训练编码器依赖更粗粒度、更稳定的表征，漂移更缓慢。

Conclusion: 这些观察结果为在面临时间漂移的真实系统中选择架构提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Real-world data distributions evolve over time, inducing temporal distribution shift that can substantially degrade the reliability of deployed machine learning systems. However, the extent to which architectural choices and their associated inductive biases affect temporal robustness remains insufficiently understood.
  We present a systematic empirical comparison of temporal robustness across three heterogeneous, time-indexed domains encompassing image classification, multi-label text classification, and text regression tasks. Using a unified evaluation framework based on temporal drift matrices, we train models on cumulative historical data and evaluate their performance on both earlier and later time periods, thereby quantifying cross-temporal generalization. Our study spans model families ranging from simple multilayer perceptrons and convolutional networks to recurrent networks and pretrained Transformer-based encoders.
  Collectively, the results show that architectural inductive biases systematically shape temporal robustness: models whose inductive biases lead them to exploit localized, highly discriminative features attain the highest in-distribution accuracy, yet those features are often the ones that change most over time, so these models degrade fastest, while pretrained encoders that draw on coarser, more stable representations drift more gradually. These observations offer practical guidance for selecting architectures for real-world systems subject to temporal drift.

</details>


### [72] [Learning Sparsest Linear Causal DAGs with Latent Confounders via Higher-Order Cumulants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05984)
*Ming Cai,Hisayuki Hara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种有限样本方法，用于在线性非高斯acyclic模型（LvLiNGAM）中恢复最稀疏的有向无环图（DAG），无需对潜在混淆变量数量施加任何限制，实验表明该方法在有限样本下优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LvLiNGAM方法虽然渐近一致，但无法在有限样本下恢复唯一的稀疏DAG，且不能处理任意数量的潜在混淆变量。

Method: 提出了一种无需限制潜在混淆变量数量的有限样本方法，用于恢复LvLiNGAM模型中的最稀疏DAG。

Result: 仿真研究和真实数据分析表明，所提方法在有限样本表现上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了LvLiNGAM中有限样本下恢复唯一稀疏DAG的难题，且对潜在混淆变量数量无限制。

Abstract: Recovering the exact directed acyclic graph (DAG) in linear non-Gaussian acyclic models with latent confounders (LvLiNGAM) remains a challenging problem. Although LvLiNGAM is identifiable only up to an observational equivalence class, each equivalence class is characterized by a unique sparsest DAG. Recovering the sparsest DAG from finite samples, however, remains difficult. Although existing methods are asymptotically consistent, they do not provide an explicit finite-sample procedure for recovering the unique sparsest DAG, nor do they handle models with an arbitrary number of latent confounders.
  In this paper, we propose a finite-sample method for recovering the sparsest DAG without imposing any restriction on the number of latent confounders. Simulation studies and real-data analyses demonstrate that the proposed method achieves superior finite-sample performance compared with existing approaches.

</details>


### [73] [SplineNet: An Isogeometric Deep Learning Method for Complex Shells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06026)
*Shizhou Luo,Xiaodong Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种等几何深度学习方法SplineNet，利用水密样条表示（如T样条）和Bernstein多项式激活函数，实现CAD/CAE在神经网络中的紧密集成，可无数据或数据驱动地分析复杂壳体结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CAD与CAE之间需要耗时的模型/数据交换过程，缺乏统一的表示框架。同时，复杂壳体结构的设计与分析面临几何描述不精确、工作流重复等问题，亟需一种无缝集成设计与分析的方法。

Method: 采用水密样条表示（如分析适用的非结构化T样条）构建网络，利用Bézier提取技术搭建网络架构，以Bernstein多项式作为非线性激活函数。在无数据场景下，嵌入基于能量的Kirchhoff-Love壳模型作为损失项；在数据驱动场景下，作为DeepONet的主干网络提供可解释性。

Result: 通过多种数值实例验证了该方法在复杂几何壳体结构上的有效性，实现了精确几何描述、端到端分析与快速推理。

Conclusion: SplineNet实现了CAD和CAE在深度神经网络中的紧密集成，消除了传统繁琐的数据交换过程，并在无数据与数据驱动两种模式下均表现出良好的效果，尤其适用于真实世界复杂几何场景。

Abstract: We present a novel isogeometric deep learning method, termed SplineNet, for the seamless design and analysis of shell structures with complex geometries. The proposed approach is built upon watertight spline representations, e.g., analysis-suitable unstructured T-splines, and features exact geometric descriptions of Computer-Aided Design (CAD) models in neural networks. Bézier extraction is used to build the network architecture, where Bernstein polynomials serve as the nonlinear activation functions. SplineNet can be applied in a data-free or data-driven way. In the data-free case, energy-based formulations can be naturally incorporated as loss terms, which fulfill the need of Computer-Aided Engineering (CAE) and can be accurately calculated. In particular, the Kirchhoff--Love (KL) model is adopted to solve for the mechanical behaviors of shell structures. This way, CAD and CAE can be tightly integrated in a deep neural network without the time-consuming model/data exchange process. In the data-driven case, SplineNet can be used as the trunk net of Deep Operator Networks (DeepONet) to provide interpretability. Given such a trained network and unseen input data, results can be immediately obtained without retraining the network or repeatedly performing the traditional workflow for analysis. In the end, a variety of numerical examples are studied to demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method, especially when real-world complex geometries are involved.

</details>


### [74] [Scalable Perturbation Learning for Online Self-Supervised Echo State Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06079)
*Taiki Yamada,Kantaro Fujiwara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于扰动的在线自监督学习方法，通过将学习限制在输入相关的低维分量，解决了回声状态网络中扰动方差随储层规模增长的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能系统需要在现实约束下同时满足三个要求：自监督学习（自主适应）、在线学习（顺序适应）和基于扰动的学习（内存高效）。然而，对于高维系统，扰动学习的方差会随着扰动变量维度的增加而增长，使得这些要求相互冲突。

Method: 对自监督学习代价函数进行正交分解，将其拆分为输入相关分量和由固定ESN参数决定的冗余分量；仅对输入相关分量进行扰动，从而将有效扰动维度从储层维度降至输入维度。

Result: 所提方法在保持自监督适应、在线学习和标量反馈扰动学习的同时，避免了随储层规模增长的方差问题。

Conclusion: 提出了一种可扩展且兼容硬件的学习设计原则：在线学习应限制在动态必要的低维目标分量上。

Abstract: Intelligent systems should not only solve tasks but also adapt under real-world constraints. Autonomous adaptation via self-supervised learning, sequential adaptation via online learning, and memory-efficient implementation via perturbation-based learning are important requirements for such systems. However, these requirements are generally in tension for high-dimensional systems, because perturbation-based learning suffers from variance that grows with the dimension of the perturbed variables.
  In this study, we focus on echo state networks (ESNs), where this tension naturally arises in large reservoirs. We propose a perturbation-based learning rule for online self-supervised learning in ESNs. The proposed rule is derived from an orthogonal decomposition of the self-supervised learning cost, which separates an input-dependent component from a redundant component determined by the fixed ESN parameters. By perturbing only the input-dependent component, the effective perturbation dimension is reduced from the reservoir dimension to the input dimension.
  Thus, the proposed method preserves self-supervised adaptation, online learning, and scalar-feedback perturbation learning, while avoiding reservoir-size-dependent variance growth. This suggests a design principle for scalable and hardware-compatible learning: online learning should be restricted to the dynamically necessary low-dimensional component of the objective.

</details>


### [75] [Modeling Normal Is All You Need: Joint Latent Clustering for Anomaly Detection in Multimodal Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06094)
*Alexander Apartsin,Yehudit Aperstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对CPS故障检测，提出MIIM假设（多模态、隐含、不平衡的多重性），采用联合学习潜在表示与高斯混合聚类的方法，并在严格公平协议下评估，在三个真实数据集上显著优于现有深度检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPS故障过于稀少且不具代表性，难以直接建模故障；标准点调整评估会奖励从不检测的检测器。需要一种基于正常行为建模、且评估协议公平的方法。

Method: 提出MIIM假设（A1-A10）；联合学习潜在表示与显式高斯混合模型聚类；在潜在空间而非全局密度或重构残差上评分；采用原始点级指标（无点调整）、困难度分层、流行度匹配F1、仅训练正常数据校准的公平评估协议。

Result: 在WADI、HAI、SKAB三个数据集上，所提检测器在综合列和困难相关/动态故障列均最优，困难子集AUROC分别达0.831、0.726、0.610；在MIIM假设针对的多模态数据集上优势最大，在近单模态数据集上优势最小；三个深度基线（USAD, TranAD, GDN）在困难子集上均失效。

Conclusion: MIIM假设集、困难分层公平协议、仅潜在空间评分（丢弃重构）是主要方法论贡献；结果验证了多模态性是CPS正常行为的关键特征，现有方法因忽略此结构而在困难故障上失效。

Abstract: Faults on a cyber-physical system (CPS) are too rare and unrepresentative to characterise, or even to select a model on, so detection must instead model normal behaviour; the standard point-adjusted evaluation, however, rewards detectors that never do. CPS normal behaviour is the union of many imbalanced, curved, thin-fringed operating regimes rather than a single blob; we state this structure as ten assumptions (A1-A10), abbreviated Massive, Implicit, Imbalanced Multimodality (MIIM). We model the normal law with a jointly learned latent representation plus explicit Gaussian-mixture mode clustering, scored in the latent rather than by a global density or a reconstruction residual, and evaluate under a deliberately fair protocol: raw point-wise metrics with no point adjustment, a trivial-detector difficulty split, prevalence-matched F1, and train-normal-only calibration. On three real CPS datasets (WADI, HAI, SKAB), the detector wins both the combined column and the difficult correlation/dynamics-fault column on all three, reaching difficult-subset AUROC 0.831 on HAI, 0.726 on WADI, and 0.610 on SKAB. The margin is largest on the two multimodal datasets the MIIM assumptions target and slimmest on the near-unimodal one, tracking multimodality as the thesis predicts, and it holds against three deep detectors (USAD, TranAD, GDN) re-computed with the same raw metrics, all of which collapse on the difficult subset. The methodological contributions are the MIIM assumption set, the difficulty-stratified fair protocol, and a latent-only score that drops reconstruction because a flexible decoder rebuilds the hard faults faithfully.

</details>


### [76] [x-Prediction Is All You Need:Training-Free Accelerated Generation via Endpoint Decodability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06114)
*Xin Peng,Ang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出**截断跳跃采样（TJS）**方法，利用扩散/流匹配模型中$x$-预测的端点可解码性，在无需重新训练、蒸馏或架构修改的情况下，将ODE采样所需的NFE减少20-70%，且质量几乎无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散和流匹配模型的ODE采样器需要数十到数百次神经函数评估（NFE），而现有加速方法（重新训练、蒸馏、轨迹重设计）成本高昂。作者希望探索一条无需额外训练成本即可加速推理的路线。

Method: 形式化定义了**端点可解码性（endpoint decodability）**：在标准仿射概率路径下，中间状态$x_t$及其路径速度可给出干净样本$x_0$的最小MSE估计$\mathbb{E}[x_0\mid x_t]$。基于此提出**截断跳跃采样（TJS）**：在早期退出时间$t^*$停止ODE求解，直接返回解码得到的$x_0$。

Result: 在SDXL、SD3.5M、Z-Image-Turbo以及三个类别条件基准上，TJS将NFE减少20-70%，且生成质量与完整采样几乎匹配。同时证明了端点预测无需轨迹拉直即可有效工作。

Conclusion: TJS是一种无需重新训练、蒸馏或架构修改的推理加速方法，通过利用ODE采样过程中固有的端点可解码性实现高效加速，为扩散模型推理加速提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow matching models generate high-quality samples, but their ODE samplers often need tens to hundreds of neural function evaluations (NFEs). This remains a practical challenge for released checkpoints, since many accelerators require additional design choices and training cost through retraining, distillation, or trajectory redesign. We investigate a different route based on $x$-prediction. During sampling, standard affine probability paths already expose $x_0$ information: an intermediate state and its path velocity determine a principled estimate of the clean sample. We formalize this property as \textbf{endpoint decodability} and show that the decoder is the minimum-MSE estimator $\mathbb{E}[x_0\mid x_t]$ under the usual $\ell_2$ objective. This yields \textbf{Truncated Jump Sampling} (TJS): stop the ODE at an early-exit time $t^*$ and return the decoded $x_0$. TJS requires no retraining, distillation, or architecture change. Across SDXL, SD3.5M, Z-Image-Turbo, and three class-conditional benchmarks, it reduces NFEs by 20--70\% with near-matched quality. The analysis also shows why endpoint prediction can work without straightening the trajectory, providing inference acceleration without trajectory redesign.

</details>


### [77] [Self-Supervised Implicit CEST Reconstruction via Physics-Informed Lorentz Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06132)
*Dexuan Li,Yupeng Wu,Chenglong Wang,Hanlin Liu,Hui Zhen,Jianqi Li,Guang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Lorentz Encoding (LE)框架，通过物理约束的隐式神经表示实现CEST MRI的快速高分辨率重建，在39点采样下达到57.58dB PSNR和0.9994 SSIM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CEST MRI采集时间长，稀疏采样重建高分辨率Z谱是一个不适定逆问题。传统插值和通用隐式神经表示缺乏物理约束，会产生频谱伪影和非物理信号。

Method: 提出Lorentz Encoding (LE)框架，将CEST重建构建为自监督连续坐标学习任务。LE将稀疏坐标投影到由参数化Lorentzian分布与可学习基函数组合控制的物理约束空间，替代通用位置编码，正则化连续频谱映射。

Result: 在人脑活体实验中，39点采样策略下LE达到57.58dB PSNR和0.9994 SSIM，显著优于现有方法。学习到的编码在潜在空间中形成连续几何有序轨迹，实现准确的APT、NOE、MT定量代谢物映射。

Conclusion: LE通过物理信息编码有效降低了噪声并确保与物理模型的一致性，为CEST MRI加速采集提供了有效的自监督重建方案。

Abstract: Multi-Pool Chemical Exchange Saturation Transfer (CEST) MRI provides valuable metabolic information but is clinically limited by long acquisition times. Although sparse sampling reduces scanning time, reconstructing high-resolution Z-spectra from limited data remains an ill-posed inverse problem. Conventional interpolation and generic Implicit Neural Rep-resentations (INRs) often lack physical constraints, leading to spectral artifacts and physically invalid signals. To address this, we propose Lorentz Encoding (LE), a physics-informed framework that formulates CEST reconstruction as a self-supervised reconstruction task via implicit continuous coordinate learning. Unlike generic positional encodings, LE regularizes the continuous spectral mapping by projecting sparse coordinates into a physically constrained space governed by a combination of parametric Lorentzian profiles with learnable basis functions. This mechanism effectively reduces noise and enforces consistency with physical models. Experiments on in vivo human brain data demonstrate that LE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods. Specifically, under a 39-point sampling strategy, LE achieves a PSNR of 57.58 dB and an SSIM of 0.9994. Furthermore, the learned physics-informed encodings form a continuous, geometrically ordered trajectory in the latent space, ensuring accurate quantitative metabo-lite mapping (APT, NOE, MT).

</details>


### [78] [KVpop -- Key-Value Cache Compression with Predictive Online Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05061)
*Lukas Hauzenberger,Niklas Schmidinger,Anamaria-Roberta Hartl,David Stap,Thomas Schmied,Sebastian Böck,Günter Klambauer,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KVpop是一种通过学习未来注意力信号来指导KV缓存驱逐策略的方法，在75%和88%压缩率下分别保留了98%和97%的全注意力性能，显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV缓存增长是自回归解码的主要瓶颈，现有驱逐方法依赖静态启发式或代理分数，无法准确追踪未来token的效用，导致驱逐决策脆弱。

Method: 提出KVpop，通过直接监督保留/丢弃决策来学习固定预算的KV驱逐策略。使用新颖的未来注意力目标训练评分器，并引入延迟记忆评分器，推迟固定步数后评分以利用近期未来上下文。

Result: 在AIME和HMMT数学推理任务上，Qwen3-4B在75%压缩率下保留98%全注意力性能，88%压缩率下保留97%；Qwen3-8B表现更优，接近教师模型性能。

Conclusion: 使用未来注意力信号监督驱逐策略可以有效降低内存成本，同时保持生成质量。

Abstract: Key-value (KV) cache growth is a major bottleneck in autoregressive decoding, as memory and bandwidth scale linearly with context length. Existing KV eviction methods often rely on static heuristics or proxy scores, which poorly track future token utility and cause brittle eviction as relevance shifts. To address this, we introduce KVpop, which learns a fixed-budget KV eviction policy by directly supervising the keep-or-drop decision. The scorer is trained against a novel future-attention target, computed efficiently without materializing dense attention maps. We further introduce a delayed memory-based scorer that, uniquely among learned eviction methods, defers scoring for a fixed number of steps to exploit near-future context. On AIME and HMMT mathematical reasoning, KVpop retains 98% of full-attention performance on Qwen3-4B at 75% KV cache compression and 97% at 88% compression, consistently outperforming established eviction baselines. Qwen3-8B shows even stronger results, reaching near-full teacher performance. These results show that supervising eviction with future-attention signals cuts memory costs while maintaining quality.

</details>


### [79] [Leveraging Extragradient for Effective Sharpness-Aware Minimization in Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06151)
*Yao Fu,Chunxia Zhang,Junmin Liu,Yihang Jin,Haishan Ye,Yuanao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EISAM优化器，通过外梯度技术改进SAM，采用两步更新（预测+扰动）寻找更平坦的极小值，在多个基准数据集上超越SGD、Adam和SAM，且对扰动半径不敏感，理论分析证明其收紧泛化边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统优化器（如SGD）易收敛到尖锐极小值导致过拟合，SAM虽能寻找平坦极小值但性能仍有提升空间，且对扰动半径敏感、调参困难。

Method: 提出外梯度启发式锐度感知最小化（EISAM），采用两步更新：第一步（预测步）探索损失景观几何结构，第二步（扰动步）结合基础优化器细化更新。

Result: 在多个基准数据集和不同架构下，EISAM在测试准确率和训练效率上一致优于SGD、Adam和SAM；对扰动半径的敏感性降低，鲁棒性增强。

Conclusion: EISAM是一种鲁棒、可扩展且广泛适用的优化方案，通过将参数导向曲率更小的平坦极小值来收紧泛化边界，推动了深度学习优化理论与实践的进步。

Abstract: Generalization remains a pivotal challenge in deep learning, where traditional optimizers like Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD) often converge to sharp minima, leading to overfitting and reduced performance on unseen data. Building on Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM), for seeking flat minima associated with improved generalization, we propose the Extragradient-Inspired Sharpness-Aware Minimization (EISAM), a novel optimizer that enhances generalization via the extragradient technique. EISAM uses a two-step update process: a prediction step investigating the geometry of the loss landscape and a perturbation step that refines updates with a base optimizer. This approach achieves better generalization performance than SAM. Crucially, EISAM reduces sensitivity to the perturbation radius, enhancing robustness, and simplifying the tuning across diverse settings. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that EISAM consistently outperforms SGD, Adaptive Moment Estimation (Adam), and SAM in test accuracy and training efficiency across various architectures. Theoretical analysis further confirms that EISAM tightens the generalization bound by steering parameters toward flatter minima with reduced curvature. Accompanied by a thorough hyperparameter analysis, EISAM offers practical tuning guidance, establishing it as a robust, scalable, and broadly applicable optimization solution that advances both the theory and practice in deep learning.

</details>


### [80] [X-FEMR: A Token-level Explainable Approach for Electronic Health Records Foundation Models using Transformer-based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06163)
*Jie Huang,Pengfei Yin,Zihan Xu,Daniel Capurro,Mike Conway,Ting Dang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个针对电子健康记录基础模型（FEMRs）的token级可解释性方法，通过训练Transformer代理模型来近似FEMR行为，识别关键token，并引入临床对齐度量评估解释的临床相关性，实验表明代理模型能有效逼近FEMR预测且解释结果与临床知识高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FEMRs在临床预测任务中表现优异，但作为黑盒模型存在偏见、可解释性差和临床信任度低的问题，缺乏对其内部决策机制的理解。

Method: 训练一个基于Transformer的代理模型，在两项预测任务上使用FEMR的输入-输出对进行训练，近似其行为并保留时间动态性；识别最具影响力的token；提出临床对齐度量来量化代理模型关键token与临床验证特征之间的一致性。

Result: 代理模型能够紧密逼近FEMR的预测结果；token级解释与临床知识具有良好的对齐性。

Conclusion: 该方法为可解释且可信的临床AI提供了实用框架，有助于提升FEMRs在临床环境中的透明度和可靠性。

Abstract: Foundation Models for Electronic Health Records (FEMRs) are pretrained on large-scale structured patient data, enabling them to convert longitudinal patient trajectories into generalizable representations for diverse clinical prediction tasks. Despite their effectiveness, FEMRs remain black-box models, raising concerns about bias, interpretability, and clinical trust. To address this, we propose the first token-level explainability approach for FEMRs. We train a Transformer-based surrogate model on input-output pairs from the FEMR across two prediction tasks, approximating its behavior while preserving temporal dynamics. We identify the most influential tokens, providing insights into how FEMRs leverage different aspects of patient history for predictions. To evaluate clinical relevance, we introduce a novel clinical alignment metric that quantifies the correspondence between the surrogate model's key tokens and clinically validated features. Our results demonstrate that the surrogate closely approximates FEMR predictions and that token-level explanations align well with clinical knowledge, offering a practical framework for interpretable and trustworthy clinical AI.

</details>


### [81] [Canopy: A Heterograph Foundation Model for Metabolic Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06224)
*Jake Bowden,Laurence Legon,Satnam Surae*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Canopy是一个异构图表征学习框架，整合10个数据源构建包含690万节点、13种节点类型和34种边类型的知识图谱，利用领域专用基础模型编码节点特征，通过四种自监督任务预训练异构图表征，在发酵滴度预测任务上显著优于传统基线方法（R²=0.41 vs 0.24）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代谢工程计算方法存在两大局限：基于化学计量约束的模型无法从实验数据中学习，而表格型机器学习方法丢弃了生物知识的图结构关系。需要一种能够统一整合多源异构数据、保留生物知识结构关系并在下游任务中取得更好预测性能的方法。

Method: Canopy构建包含6.9M节点（13种类型）和34种边类型的异构图知识图谱，集成10个公共和专有数据源。利用ESM-2（蛋白序列）、MoLFormer（化学SMILES）和PubMedBERT（生物文本）编码多模态节点特征。采用Heterogeneous Graph Transformer作为骨干网络，结合SignNet位置编码、Jumping Knowledge聚合和虚拟节点技术，通过链接预测、掩码节点建模、距离预测和对比实验聚类四个自监督任务进行预训练，使用同方差不确定性加权平衡多任务损失。

Result: 在发酵滴度预测任务中，冻结的Canopy嵌入配合轻量级探测头实现R²=0.41，优于表格型基线（最佳R²=0.24）以及同构图GNN变体。

Conclusion: 将异构图表征学习与多模态基础模型结合，能够有效整合生物知识图谱的结构信息和多源数据，显著提升代谢工程中关键表型（如发酵滴度）的预测能力，为数据驱动的菌株工程设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Designing microbial strains that produce high-value chemicals at commercially viable titers remains a central challenge in metabolic engineering. Existing computational approaches either rely on stoichiometric constraint-based models that cannot learn from experimental data, or apply tabular machine learning to hand-crafted features that discard the relational structure of biological knowledge. We present Canopy, a heterogeneous graph foundation model that integrates ten public and proprietary data sources into a unified knowledge graph (KG) of 6.9M nodes across 13 types and 34 edge types, covering genes, proteins, metabolites, reactions, pathways, strains, and fermentation experiments. Node features are encoded through domain-specific foundation models (ESM-2 for protein sequences, MoLFormer for chemical SMILES, and PubMedBERT for biomedical text), yielding a multi-modal representation within a single graph. We pretrain a Heterogeneous Graph Transformer (HGT) augmented with SignNet positional encodings, Jumping Knowledge aggregation, and virtual nodes using four self-supervised objectives (link prediction, masked node modelling, distance prediction, and contrastive experiment clustering), balanced via learned homoscedastic uncertainty weighting. On the downstream task of fermentation titer prediction, frozen Canopy embeddings achieve $R^{2} = 0.41$ with a lightweight probe, outperforming tabular baselines (best $R^{2} = 0.24$) and homogeneous GNN variants.

</details>


### [82] [Physics-Informed Neural Embeddings of PDE Solution Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06348)
*Raul Jimenez,Svitlana Mayboroda,Pavlos Protopapas,Leonid Sarieddine,David N. Spergel,Pedro Tarancón-Álvarez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理信息的框架，用于学习偏微分方程解族的有限维嵌入。该方法使用多头物理信息神经网络，共享主体学习解空间的潜在流形，线性头则重构不同初始条件对应的解。头部正交化惩罚消除了潜在表示的退化，并稳定了主成分谱。应用于一维粘性Burgers方程，在潜在维度为20时，仅2-4个主成分即可捕获约95%的潜在空间方差。还提出基于傅里叶壳的频率分析方法，建立了鲁棒的可观测解流形几何特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的PDE降阶模型和嵌入学习方法缺乏物理一致性，难以在保持可解释性的同时学习解空间的结构化低维表示。本文旨在开发一种物理嵌入方法，使得学到的潜在表示具有明确的物理意义、可重复性，并能揭示解空间的内在几何结构。

Method: 采用多头物理信息神经网络架构：(1) 共享神经网络主体学习潜在流形；(2) 多个线性头对应不同初始条件重构解；(3) 引入头部正交化惩罚项消除潜在表示的简并性；(4) 提出傅里叶壳分解方法分析主成分的频域贡献分布。

Result: 对于一维Burgers方程，在潜在维度nb=20时，2-4个主成分捕获约95%的潜在方差，4-7个主成分捕获约99%。热方程和波动方程验证了同样的压缩特性。傅里叶壳频率分析在不同训练运行间保持可重复性，表明学到的谱特征和主成分是解流形几何的稳健可观测量。

Conclusion: 该方法成功建立了具有物理可解释性的解流形低维嵌入框架，正交化惩罚和傅里叶壳分析为解空间的几何结构提供了鲁棒的量化工具，可广泛应用于多种PDE系统。

Abstract: We introduce a physics-informed framework for learning finite-dimensional embeddings of solution families of partial differential equations. The method uses a multihead Physics-Informed Neural Network in which a shared body learns a latent manifold representing the solution space, while linear heads reconstruct individual solutions associated with different initial conditions. A head-orthogonalization penalty removes degeneracies in the latent representation and stabilizes the principal-component spectrum across training realizations. Because the initial condition is built into the network output by construction, these principal components measure the additional variability the network learns on top of the initial profile, not the full solution itself. We apply the method to the one-dimensional viscous Burgers equation, with the heat and wave equations as robustness checks. For a latent dimension $n_b=20$, the learned manifolds exhibit pronounced effective dimensional reduction: for Burgers dynamics, only $2$-$4$ principal components capture about $95\%$ of the latent-space variance, while $4$-$7$ capture about $99\%$, depending on the initial-condition family; the same qualitative compression holds for the heat and wave equations. We also split the wavenumber axis into bands (``Fourier shells'') and measure how much each band contributes to every principal component. The resulting frequency profile is invariant under the change-of-basis freedom that the orthogonalization penalty leaves in the latent space, and is therefore reproducible across independent training runs. More broadly, this establishes the learned spectral profiles and principal components as robust observables of solution-manifold geometry.

</details>


### [83] [TILDE: TILt-based Distributional Erasure for Concept Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06432)
*Naveen George,Naoki Murata,Yuhta Takida,Konda Reddy Mopuri,Yuki Mitsufuji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TILDE将概念遗忘形式化为分布对齐问题，通过能量倾斜的目标分布和残差∇-GFlowNet训练，在有效遗忘目标概念的同时保持生成质量和多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念遗忘方法虽能有效移除目标概念，但缺乏对保留生成质量、多样性和语义覆盖的明确规范，导致遗忘后的模型分布无法逼近仅用良性数据从头训练的理想模型。

Method: 将概念遗忘建模为分布对齐问题，目标是在遗忘约束下最小化与预训练模型的KL散度；通过能量倾斜构造锚定自由的遗忘目标分布；采用残差∇-GFlowNet学习预训练扩散模型的分数修正项。

Result: 在目标概念、艺术风格和角色等遗忘任务上，TILDE在实现强遗忘效果的同时，在保留质量和分布保真度上优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: TILDE通过显式定义遗忘的分布目标，为扩散模型概念遗忘提供了一种兼顾遗忘效果与生成保留性能的理论框架与实用方法。

Abstract: Concept unlearning in text-to-image diffusion models is critical for safe and practical deployment: with rising privacy concerns, copyright disputes, trademark constraints, and safety regulations, deployed systems must be able to suppress unwanted concepts after training. Existing methods often remove the target concept effectively, but practical unlearning also requires an equally fundamental property: the unlearned model should retain quality, diversity, and semantic coverage on benign generation. The gold standard is a retain-only model trained from scratch without the unwanted data. However, common erasure objectives do not specify which post-unlearning distribution should approximate this reference, leaving retention as an implicit consequence of the update rule. We propose TILDE, TILt-based Distributional Erasure, which formulates concept unlearning as a distributional alignment problem: the desired target is the minimum-deviation conditional distribution from the pretrained model under a forgetting constraint. This energy-tilted, anchor-free target suppresses concept-expressing images while preserving benign relative mass for each prompt. We instantiate this principle with residual $\nabla$-GFlowNet training, which learns the score correction induced by the forget energy relative to the pretrained diffusion model. Across objects, artistic styles, and characters, TILDE achieves strong forgetting while improving retention and distributional fidelity over prior baselines.

</details>


### [84] [Graph Convolutional Attention: A Spectral Perspective on Graph Denoising and Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06546)
*Shervin Khalafi,Igor Krawczuk,Sergio Rozada,Charilaos Kanatsoulis,Antonio G Marques,Alejandro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出图卷积注意力（GCA）机制，证明线性注意力在图去噪任务中只能学习平均谱滤波器，存在局限性。GCA通过利用输入图频谱实现谱去噪，在理论和实验上均优于线性注意力，可替代标准图Transformer中的昂贵结构特征计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于注意力的架构（如图Transformer）在图去噪中表现良好，但对其原理性理解有限，不清楚标准注意力机制是否适合该任务。线性注意力在去噪目标下只能学习训练分布上的平均谱滤波器，无法处理图分布中频谱多样性的问题。

Method: 提出Spectral Attention概念，直接利用输入图谱；推导出其置换等变的实际实现——图卷积注意力（GCA），通过图滤波后的查询和键实现谱去噪。此外，证明softmax操作通过将噪声特征向量近似投影到干净特征空间来提供额外去噪能力。

Result: 在随机块模型上，GCA理论上匹配理想化的Spectral Attention。实验上，用GCA替换线性注意力一致提升图去噪和扩散性能，增益与频谱多样性强相关。在DiGress中，GCA无需昂贵结构特征即可匹配标准图Transformer性能，与PEARL位置编码结合时可避免显式特征分解，实现更快推理且不损失质量。

Conclusion: 线性注意力在图去噪中存在根本性局限，GCA通过谱感知机制克服该局限，为图扩散模型提供了更高效、原理清晰且经验上更优的注意力替代方案。

Abstract: Denoising graphs is a fundamental problem in graph learning and the core operation of graph diffusion models. Attention-based architectures like graph transformers have recently shown promise in denoising graphs. However, our principled understanding of attention-based graph denoising remains limited, making it unclear whether standard attention is the right mechanism for this task. Here we show that, under a denoising objective, linear attention is suboptimal and can only learn an average spectral denoising filter over the training distribution. This creates a fundamental limitation as graphs often vary spectrally across the distribution. To overcome this limitation, we introduce Spectral Attention, which directly utilizes the input graph spectrum and provably outperforms linear attention by a margin governed by the spectral diversity of the distribution. We then derive Graph Convolutional Attention (GCA), a practical and permutation-equivariant realization of this idea that implements spectral denoising through graph-filtered queries and keys. For stochastic block models, GCA provably matches the idealized Spectral Attention mechanism. We further show that the softmax operation, that follows the attention, provides additional denoising by approximately projecting noisy eigenvectors onto the clean eigenspace. Empirically, replacing linear attention with GCA consistently improves graph denoising and diffusion on synthetic and real datasets, with gains strongly correlated with spectral diversity. In DiGress, GCA matches standard graph-transformer performance without computing expensive structural features, and when combined with the recently proposed PEARL positional encodings, avoids explicit eigendecomposition computations resulting in faster inference without degrading quality. The code can be found here: github.com/shervinkhalafi/graph_conv_att

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [85] [Prompt-to-Paper: Agentic AI System for Bioinformatics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05456)
*Ramsha Kamran,Maheera Amjad,Zartasha Mustansar,Arsalan Shaukat,Salma Sherbaz,Muhammad U. S. Khan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Prompt-to-Paper是一个多智能体框架，通过确定性检索增强生成、自主编码实验执行和八维自动质量评分器，解决了AI生成手稿的三个关键缺陷（不可验证的声明、虚构的实验结果、缺乏标准化评估框架），在五个生物信息学案例上生成了符合投稿格式的PDF，质量评分平均提升+17.96分（满分100），人工评审平均7.0/10分，每篇成本约0.31美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在端到端自动手稿生成中存在三个关键缺陷：(i) 生成的声明无法确定性地锚定在可验证文献中，(ii) 实验结果经常是虚构而非实际执行的，(iii) 缺乏标准化的多维度框架来评估AI生成手稿是否达到真实出版所需的质量和严谨性。

Method: 提出Prompt-to-Paper多智能体框架，包含三个核心创新：(1) 确定性检索增强生成流水线，采用分区相关度评分和雪球引用扩展，将每个声明锚定在60-100篇论文的可验证语料库中；(2) 自主编码代理执行真实计算生物学实验，用真实数值结果替代合成输出；(3) 八维自动质量评分器，以已发表论文的近似参考统计为基准，加入显式幻觉惩罚，提供标准化可复现的质量评估。质量驱动改进循环使用上下文丰富的修订器，每次迭代路由到三种研究人员行为之一，每十次迭代触发深度研究循环重新运行实验并从更强输出重新生成手稿。

Result: 在五个生物信息学案例研究上验证，所有五个案例均生成了零越界引用的投稿格式PDF。改进循环将手稿质量平均提升+17.96分（0-100分制，最高+26.04分）。人工评审员给五篇手稿的平均评分为7.0/10分。每篇完整手稿的生产成本约为0.31美元。

Conclusion: Prompt-to-Paper框架通过确定性文献锚定、真实实验执行和标准化质量评估，有效解决了AI生成手稿的关键缺陷，以极低成本（每篇0.31美元）生成了达到人工评审7.0/10分的高质量学术手稿，为AI辅助学术写作提供了可行的标准化方案。

Abstract: While recent advances in large language models have enabled end-to-end automated manuscript generation, existing systems suffer from three critical deficiencies: (i) generated claims are not deterministically grounded in verifiable literature, (ii) experimental results are frequently fabricated rather than executed, and (iii) there exists no standardized, multi-dimensional framework to assess whether AI-generated manuscripts meet the quality and rigor required for real-world publication. We present Prompt-to-Paper, a multi-agent framework that directly addresses this evaluation gap through three integrated innovations. First, a deterministic retrieval-augmented generation pipeline with section-aware relevance scoring and snowball citation expansion grounds every claim in a verifiable corpus of 60--100 papers. Second, an autonomous coding agent executes real computational biology experiments replacing synthetic outputs with genuine numerical results. Third, an eight-dimensional automated quality scorer, benchmarked with approximate reference statistics from published papers and augmented with explicit hallucination penalties, provides standardized, reproducible quality assessments. The quality-driven improvement loop uses a context-rich reviser that routes each iteration to one of three researcher actions and fires a deep research cycle every ten iterations to re-run experiments and re-manuscript from stronger outputs. We validate the system on five bioinformatics case studies; all five cases compiled submission-formatted PDFs with zero out-of-range citations. The improvement loop raises manuscript quality by an average of +17.96 points on a 0--100 scale (maximum +26.04. As partial external checks, a human reviewer scored the five manuscripts at an average of 7.0 out of 10. Complete manuscripts are produced at approximately 0.31 USD per paper.

</details>


### [86] [From Graphs to Gradients: Physics-Inspired Structural Attribution for Cyber-Physical IoT Systems and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05563)
*Spyridon Evangelatos,Christos Diou,Georgios Th. Papadopoulos,Evangelos Markakis,Panagiotis Sarigiannidis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于统计力学的、无向能量表示框架，用于在无需显式恢复有向因果图的情况下，对大规模混合型网络物理系统（如工业物联网）进行依赖感知的归因分析，在准确率、鲁棒性和可扩展性上优于现有图方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可解释性方法主要捕捉输入输出间的相关性，而因果解释虽能回答干预性问题，但在大规模、混合、带反馈回路和部分可观测的网络物理系统中恢复显式有向因果结构往往不切实际。

Method: 受统计力学启发，将网络物理系统建模为基于能量的无向表示，通过分析能量景观的变异来反映各组件的影响，从而实现依赖感知的归因，并支持跨混合交互的扰动效应推理。

Result: 在工业物联网仿真测试平台上，针对混合连续和离散变量进行实验，该方法在归因准确率、鲁棒性和可扩展性方面均优于最先进的基于图的方法。

Conclusion: 该框架虽不能完全恢复系统的生成动力学，但能提供有价值的依赖感知解释，支持人类理解和下游预测/诊断任务。除工业物联网安全外，还可推广至其他高维网络物理和社会技术系统。

Abstract: Interpretable explanation methods in Artificial Intelligence aim to uncover the underlying causes and their effects, enabling a deeper understanding of why a system behaves in a certain way under different inputs. Unlike traditional explainability methods, which mainly highlight correlations between input and output variables, causal explanation focuses on interventional questions. By doing so, it provides more robust insights, helping users understand automated decisions, especially in high-risk domains. Recovering an explicit directed causal structure, however, is often impractical in large-scale, hybrid cyber-physical systems with feedback loops and partial observability. This paper introduces a novel framework inspired by statistical mechanics that instead models variable dependencies through an undirected, energy-based representation of cyber-physical IoT systems. Our approach enables rigorous dependency-aware attribution by analysing how variations in the energy landscape reflect the influence of individual components, without recovering a directed causal graph. It also supports reasoning about perturbation effects across hybrid interactions, providing reliable explanations of abnormal behaviours. We empirically examined our framework through simulations on an industrial IoT testbed with hybrid continuous and discrete variables, demonstrating higher attribution accuracy, improved robustness and better scalability than state-of-the-art graph-based approaches. While the attributions are not intended to fully recover the system's generative dynamics, they provide valuable, dependency-aware explanations supporting both human interpretation and downstream predictive and diagnostic tasks. Although demonstrated in industrial IoT security, our framework also applies to other high-dimensional cyber-physical and socio-technical systems requiring principled, structural explanations.

</details>


### [87] [CSTutorBench: Benchmarking Small Language Models as Tutors for Block-Based Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05571)
*H. Chad Lane,Bryson Kageler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CSTutorBench是一个用于评估语言模型在块编程教育场景中作为AI导师能力的基准测试，包含17个基于教学法评分的情景问题。初步发现：模型在表层标准（如词汇和语气）表现良好，但在深层教学行为（如避免答案泄漏和处理学生调试历史）上存在困难；模型家族和指令调优方式比参数量更能预测教学质量；针对性提示优化能提升大部分模型的表现。


<details>
